## Nightlife Mirror Selfie JSON Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{ "meta": { "camera": "iPhone 17 Pro", "lens": "48mm", "aspect_ratio": "9:16", "quality": "ultra photorealistic", "style": "flash mirror shot, nightlife realism, imperfect reflections" }, "scene": { "location": "luxury hotel elevator mirror", "time": "1:58 AM", "atmosphere": "post-party, quiet tension, reflective surfaces everywhere" }, "lighting": { "type": "iPhone flash + elevator LEDs", "temperature": "mixed neutral", "effect": [ "hard flash highlights", "deep shadows behind", "mirror reflections multiplying light", "slight glare on metal walls" ] }, "subjects": [ { "role": "front girl", "pose": { "position": "holding phone for mirror selfie", "hips": "shifted hard to one side" }, "expression": { "eyes": "direct camera contact", "energy": "confident, in control" } }, { "role": "back girl", "pose": { "position": "standing directly behind, slightly pressed in", "arms": "lightly touching front girl’s waist" }, "interaction": { "contact": "subtle waist touch", "tension": "possessive but playful" }, "expression": { "eyes": "looking at front girl’s reflection", "energy": "territorial, teasing" } } ], "styling": { "outfits": [ "black micro dress (front)", "dark grey crop + mini skirt (back)" ], "hair": "contrasting styles (sleek vs messy ponytail)" }, "camera": { "angle": "mirror eye-level", "distance": "mid-body", "imperfection": "flash bloom + smudged mirror" }, "post_processing": { "editing": "minimal", "contrast": "high", "grain": "visible", "skin": "real texture" } }
```

## Sydney Sweeney mirror selfie in athletic wear

```
The prompt is already in English. A translation would be identical to the original content. The prompt describes a mirror selfie photograph taken with an iPhone 16 Pro front camera, featuring a young Caucasian woman in her early 20s with an athletic build, blonde hair, and a playful expression. She is seated sideways on a white stool wearing a white cropped ribbed cotton top and light periwinkle blue high-waisted ruched yoga shorts. The photo includes detailed specifications for the subject's appearance, clothing physics, skin texture, biological features, environmental elements, lighting, camera settings, and post-processing effects to create a realistic amateur smartphone mirror selfie aesthetic.
```

## Backless Silk Dress Photo Prompt

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create the person from the reference image from the photo, without changing her face, features, appearance, age, and individuality. She is in an elegant evening dress with an open back, the fabric emphasizes thin skin with a natural glow, folds and the luster of silk give depth. Leans on an unprocessed stone counter with a rough texture contrasting with the dress and skin. The muted lounge light creates soft shadows, emphasizing the curves of the back and the transitions of light on the dress and stone. Hair in a bun with loose strands, giving sophistication and restraint. A long sparkling diamond earring descends down the back, catching the light and creating a shimmer. high resolution photo with grainy film texture and high contrast, in the style of classic noir and fashion editorial photography, with a cold deep visual style. light enhances the person from the reference image looks half-turned, looking back. Strictly preserve the face, identity and natural appearance of the person from the reference image from the original. Mega-drawn skin every pore color photo
```

## Ultra-Realistic Winking Portrait with Playful Expression

> Fotogerçekçi, stüdyo ışıklı moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic 3:4 vertical portrait photograph, mid-shot slightly cropped below the chest. Camera positioned slightly above eye level with a subtle downward tilt. The subject is centered and leaning slightly forward toward the camera, with sharp focus on the face and a shallow depth of field.

A woman in her mid-20s with light to medium warm beige skin and subtle peach undertones, smooth natural texture with a soft glow. She has an oval face with a softly defined jawline, almond-shaped medium-sized greenish-hazel eyes, one eye open and the other winking. Eyelashes are medium length and slightly curled, eyebrows are natural, medium thickness, softly arched and brown.

She has a straight nose with a slightly rounded tip and full lips (especially the lower lip) in a natural warm pink tone. Her expression is playful, flirty, and casual, with her tongue slightly sticking out to one side. Cheeks are softly contoured with natural fullness and a light rosy blush.

Her hair is medium brown with warm undertones, long past the shoulders, slightly wavy, side-parted, and loosely draped over one shoulder with a natural flow.

She is wearing a fitted black off-shoulder dress made of soft stretchy fabric, tight and body-hugging, with a wide neckline exposing collarbones and shoulders.

Accessories include long thin gold dangling earrings and layered gold necklaces: one short thin chain and one slightly longer chain with a small heart-shaped pendant.

Lighting is soft studio lighting, frontal with slight side emphasis, evenly diffused with minimal soft shadows enhancing facial contours.

Background is a plain light beige/off-white smooth seamless studio backdrop with no objects or distractions.

Color palette includes black, warm beige, gold, and brown, with moderate contrast between dark clothing and light background, maintaining a warm natural tone.

Style is modern fashion/editorial portrait photography with a playful, confident, intimate mood. Light retouching only, preserving natural skin texture.

Shot with a portrait lens (50mm–85mm look), shallow depth of field, high facial detail, minimal noise.
```

## Cinematic Hollywood-Style Street Market Walk

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a highly cinematic, Hollywood-style photo of a person using the face from the uploaded image. The person is walking confidently through a busy street market at night filled with colorful lights, neon signs, and glowing stalls. The camera captures a mid-shot from slightly low angle, giving a powerful and dramatic feel. People around are moving in motion blur, creating a sense of energy and chaos, while the main person is sharp and in focus. Warm and cool lights mix together, reflecting on the ground and surroundings. Light smoke and atmosphere enhance the depth of the scene. The person is wearing stylish modern clothing like a jacket, t-shirt, jeans, and sneakers. Cinematic lighting, dramatic contrast, vibrant colors, shallow depth of field, ultra realistic, movie-scene composition, 8k, Hollywood film look.
```

## Cinematic photo of a woman on a subway platform with motion blur

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic cinematic photo of a woman using the face from the uploaded image. She is standing on a subway platform while a train passes behind her at high speed, creating motion blur. The woman is still and in sharp focus, looking slightly to the side with a calm and confident expression. She is holding a bouquet of flowers wrapped in paper in one hand. The outfit is stylish and modern - an oversized blazer, short skirt or shorts, and knee-high boots. The lighting is soft but cinematic, with natural indoor tones from the station lights. The background shows the moving train blurred with streaks of motion. The floor reflects a bit of light, adding depth. Cinematic photography, motion blur effect, atmospheric depth, realistic shadows, high detail, aesthetic composition.
```

## Cinematic Close-Up Portrait with Lens Flares

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a soft, cinematic close-up portrait of a person using the face from the uploaded image. The framing is tight on the face (head and shoulders), with the person looking slightly upward and toward the light with a calm, natural expression. The lighting is soft and directional, casting gentle shadows across one side of the face. Add subtle rainbow-like lens flares and light streaks passing across the face, creating a dreamy and artistic effect. The background is minimal, smooth, and out of focus. The overall color tone is warm with slight teal highlights, giving a cinematic color grade. Skin texture should be natural and detailed. Soft focus, shallow depth of field, cinematic lighting, light leaks, atmospheric glow, ultra realistic yet artistic photography.
```

## Cinematic Portrait in Kashmir Mustard Field

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A highly realistic cinematic portrait of a young man standing in a vast mustard field in Kashmir during the spring season. The field is filled with bright yellow mustard blooms stretching endlessly into the distance, gently swaying in a soft breeze. Snow capped mountains are faintly visible far in the background under a clear blue sky, adding depth and a natural Kashmiri landscape feel.

The young man stands relaxed among the tall mustard plants, slightly brushing the flowers with his hand while looking calmly toward the horizon. His expression is peaceful and natural. His hair moves softly in the breeze, adding a sense of motion.

He is wearing a simple light outfit, a white shirt with rolled sleeves and dark pants, creating a clean contrast against the vibrant yellow flowers. The sunlight falls warmly across his face and shoulders, creating natural highlights and soft shadows with realistic skin texture.

The scene feels fresh and bright with soft natural daylight illuminating the field. Small details like moving flowers, distant hills, and light wind add realism.

Captured like professional outdoor photography using a DSLR camera with an 85mm lens. Shallow depth of field keeps the young man in sharp focus while the mustard field softly blurs in the background. Ultra photorealistic lighting, cinematic color grading, high dynamic range, extremely detailed, 8k resolution.
```

In the second image,

The woman in blue has her eyes closed with a gentle smile, cradling a grumpy-faced blue plush bear. Other on the right, leans toward her companion with a playful expression, sticking her tongue out while wearing a fuzzy lavender paw-glove. ## Calm in Chaos Street Photo Prompt for Nano Banana 2

> Sinematik, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic photo of a person using the face from the uploaded image. The person is standing still in the middle of a busy street or public place, looking calm and confident. Many people are moving around them, but they appear blurred because of motion, while the main person is sharp and in focus. The camera is at eye level, centered on the person. The background looks like a city with soft lights and buildings. The person is wearing simple stylish clothes like a t-shirt, jacket, jeans, and sneakers. The lighting is natural and slightly cinematic with soft shadows. The overall scene should feel calm in the middle of chaos, with clear focus on the person and blurred crowd movement around.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Café Portrait Prompt for Nano Banana 2

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic full-body portrait of a young man seated on a modern wooden lounge chair inside a cozy, aesthetically pleasing café. He is posed with legs crossed at the knee and arms folded across his chest, conveying a relaxed yet confident demeanor. His posture is natural and composed, with his head slightly turned to the side, expressing a calm, introspective mood.
The subject features styled, voluminous hair and a full, well-groomed beard. He is dressed in a beige short-sleeve shirt with a subtle palm print pattern, paired with dark blue jeans, clean white sneakers, and a sleek black smartwatch, creating a contemporary casual look.
The environment is warmly lit with ambient golden tones, enhanced by soft, out-of-focus string lights in the background producing a pleasing bokeh effect. The scene should have a shallow depth of field to isolate the subject while maintaining a cinematic atmosphere.
Captured with a 50mm lens perspective, emphasizing natural proportions and realism. Ensure extremely high detail (8K resolution), sharp focus on the subject, accurate skin texture, and lifelike rendering. Preserve the exact facial features from the reference image without distortion. Color grading should lean toward a cinematic, warm, and inviting tone.
```

## Dreamy Cinematic Cloud Walk Prompt for Nano Banana 2

> Sinematik, minimal görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic cinematic photo of a person using the face from the uploaded image. The person is walking calmly through soft white clouds as if they are above the sky. The camera angle is slightly low and in front, capturing the person mid-walk. The clouds look fluffy and glowing with soft sunlight coming through them. The sky is bright with a peaceful, dreamy feel. The person is wearing simple, stylish clothes like a t-shirt, jacket, pants, and sneakers. Their clothes and hair move slightly in a gentle breeze. The overall scene feels calm, dreamy, and cinematic with soft light, natural shadows, and a professional photography look.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Cafe Portrait with Reference Image

> Fotogerçekçi yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 3:4.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-sharp photograph taken on a modern smartphone, specifically an iPhone 15 Pro, characterized by its digital clarity and shot on a wide-angle lens at ISO 50 and f/1.8. The subject is a young woman in her early 20s, captured in a candid, mid-moment pose at a cafe, her torso angled 20 degrees to the left while her head is playfully tilted 15 degrees to the right. Following a natural weight shift, her right shoulder is slightly elevated as she holds a small dessert fork with a silver-tined tip mid-air in her right hand, while her left hand supports a white ceramic plate from beneath, showcasing one hand active and the other in a relaxed grip. Her long dark hair is styled in soft mermaid waves that drape over her right shoulder, partially covered by a cream-colored cotton canvas baseball cap featuring a delicate, hand-embroidered pink ribbon bow on the crown. Her expression is radiant yet playful, winking her right eye shut while her left eye remains wide and sparkling with a glimmering inner corner shine; a subtle micro-smirk forms on her glossy, natural pinkish-mauve lips. Her left eyebrow is naturally relaxed while the right is subtly engaged in the wink, creating a realistic facial asymmetry. The subject’s skin exhibits a healthy radiance with a rosy warmth, featuring visible pores and a clear texture with soft highlights along the bridge and tip of her nose. A subtle pink blush is applied with high placement on the cheekbones, blending into the pinkish-rosy undertones of her complexion. She wears an oversized, soft-brushed cotton plaid shirt in a grid of cerulean blue and muted beige, the fabric showing microscopic weave textures and realistic folds around the dropped shoulders. A single strand of small, luminous freshwater pearls sits delicately around her neck, and a simple gold ring is visible on her finger. In the foreground, a square artisanal cake is decorated with intricate blue and pink piped buttercream ruffles and topped with a small, round character face. The scene is set in a sunlit cafe with a warm wooden floor transitioning into a section of green and white geometric patterned tiles, with large wooden-framed windows allowing soft, diffused daylight to illuminate the scene from the front-side, creating gentle shadows under the jawline. The entire scene is rendered in incredibly sharp focus, from the intricate frosting on the cake to the wood grain in the background, without any background blur or bokeh. Digital texture is paramount, revealing crisp fab
```

## Cinematic Medium Shot with Chalkboard Background Prompt

> Sinematik bilgi grafiği üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Image from subject, cinematic medium shot with a slight low-angle perspective. The subject is leaning back against a surface, in a three-quarter profile view, gazing thoughtfully off-camera to the side. Wearing a rumpled white button-up shirt with rolled-up sleeves, a dark textured necktie loosely knotted, and brown chino pants. Standing against an expansive, worn black chalkboard covered in dense white chalk writing, diagrams, arrows, and philosophical text. The lighting is soft, diffused, and medium-key, originating from the right, creating gentle, open shadows and evenly illuminating the scene without harsh highlights. The overall image is brightly lit with moderate contrast, preserving detail in the mid-tones of the shirt and skin. Color grading is a warm, desaturated sepia with a distinct greenish-yellow tint, characteristic of aged film under warm fluorescent light. Blacks are slightly lifted but not crushed. Medium 50mm film grain is present throughout.
```

## Dark, Moody, Photorealistic Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a dark, moody, photorealistic portrait of a young man (early 20s, slim face, pale skin, soft sharp jawline, messy medium-length wavy dark hair partially covering forehead). He is seated on a simple wooden chair, body slightly turned sideways, head tilted downward, eyes cast toward his hands in a contemplative, melancholic expression. He wears a black high-neck turtleneck under a long black overcoat with soft matte fabric. Normal wavy hair.
Background is a rough, textured wall with subtle stains and uneven paint, desaturated green-gray tones. Lighting is extremely low-key, coming from a soft side source (left side), creating deep shadows that partially obscure facial features while still keeping them faintly visible. Composition is medium shot, slightly off center framing. Add heavy film grain, analog noise, subtle blur, and muted color grading.
Keep everything natural and realistic: no stylization, no extra elements, no modern sharpness. Extremely grainy.
Maintain consistent shadows, realistic proportions, and a raw cinematic atmosphere with imperfect texture and slight noise artifacts.
```

Artifacts: Intense white flash glare obscuring the device and hand, light streaking, hazy soft-focus look, visible mirror dust. Color grading: Desaturated, cool-toned, low-fi dark aesthetic with blown-out highlights. She is standing at a slight angle with a relaxed posture, arms naturally at her sides, looking upwards and to her right with a pleasant, slightly coy smile. Her expression is natural and warm, showing her top teeth.

Her skin tone is light-to-medium tanned with visible natural skin texture and a soft rosy-pink blush on her cheeks. She has clearly defined eyebrows and blue-green eyes, with a natural gaze directed upward-right. Her lips are soft pink with a subtle glossy finish.

Her hair is light blonde with subtle highlights, styled straight and mostly pulled back, with two straight strands left loose to frame her face. The hair texture is smooth and clean.

She is wearing a fitted charcoal-grey athletic unitard (one-piece romper) made of smooth, form-fitting fabric.

Her accessories are detailed and layered: a multi-strand pearl choker and a longer gold chain necklace with a medallion pendant. She wears a single large pearl earring (visible on the right ear). On her right wrist, she has stacked bracelets including multi-strand pearl, gold chain, and textured white beaded pieces. On her left wrist, she wears multiple white and light-colored beaded bracelets. She carries a light sage-green quilted leather crossbody bag with prominent gold hardware.

The environment is a clean, modern clothing store. On the left side, there is white shelving with a green t-shirt hanging on display. The floor is polished light-colored marble tile. The right side opens toward a large glass window.

Through the window, a detailed urban street scene is visible, including leafy trees and modern glass buildings.

The lighting is bright, even indoor lighting combined with natural daylight from the window, creating clean reflections and a well-lit scene.

The overall style is highly realistic, sharp, and detailed with natural colors and balanced contrast.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Portrait on a Boat

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create image: Use the attached reference image as a visual guide for the character’s appearance, maintaining strong resemblance to the person in the reference. Preserve key visual traits such as facial structure and overall proportions, while allowing small natural variations typical of real photography. The generated subject should clearly resemble the reference while remaining a natural photographic interpretation.
Aspect Ratio: 9:16.
A hyper-realistic, ultra-realistic photograph shot on a Sony A7R V with an 85mm f/1.8 lens, settings at ISO 100, shutter speed 1/500s, aperture f/2.8, capturing an upper-body to medium-long shot with a moderately shallow depth of field creating a pleasing bokeh effect. The aspect ratio is 9:16. The camera angle is slightly downward, positioned at eye-level with the seated subject. The subject, possessing a slender and graceful build with smooth, light-medium warm-toned skin displaying realistic texture, reclines on the white deck of a boat. The subject is angled approximately 20 degrees towards the camera's right. The subject's left arm is extended and gently rests on the boat's edge, supporting the upper body, fingers relaxed. The right arm is bent at the elbow, with the hand resting on the raised right knee. The left leg is extended across the boat's surface, while the right leg is bent at the knee, creating a relaxed posture. The gaze is directed straight at the camera with a calm, composed, and captivating expression, head held level. The dark brown hair is long, straight, parted slightly off-center, and cascades over the subject's left shoulder and partially down the back, with a few strands softly wind-blown around the face. The subject wears a form-fitting, black midi-length halter-neck dress made of a soft, stretchy knit fabric, featuring a high slit on the left side that exposes the upper thigh. A delicate silver pendant necklace with a small circular charm rests at the base of the subject's throat. The subject's facial style includes defined, natural-looking eyebrows, subtle eyeshadow, thin eyeliner, mascara, and soft pink lips with a hint of gloss, complemented by natural-looking foundation. The lighting is bright, natural daylight, coming from above and slightly in front, creating soft highlights on the skin and dress, with minimal, gentle shadows that sculpt the form. The color grading is vibrant and natural, with rich blue tones in the water and sky, and lush greens on the distant islands. The overall mood is tranquil, glamorous, and natural. The background features an expansive, clear blue ocean with subtle ripples and distant green, lush islands under a clear blue sky, rendered with a soft bokeh. The foreground includes the crisp white deck of the boat with hints of its structure and railings. The image has a clean, crisp, and hyper-photorealistic finish, absent of any film grain or digital noise.
```

## Voluptuous Spider-Man Cosplay Portrait

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Young woman with fair, pale skin wearing a full-body tight spandex cosplay bodysuit in a Spider-Man design. The suit features a deep red center panel on the torso and mask area with a black web pattern, and dark navy blue panels on the sides, arms, and legs. The fabric has a subtle sheen and visible texture. She has a silver septum piercing hoop in the nose. The suit fits extremely tightly, conforming like a second skin to the body's contours. She has a voluptuous hourglass figure with a very distinct waist-to-hip ratio. Bust volume is prominent and heavy, significantly above average size. The chest mass projects forward and outward, creating tension in the fabric of the bodysuit. The shape is natural and affected by gravity, not defying physics. The red pattern of the suit stretches over the volume. Realistic skin texture visible on exposed areas, with natural pores and subtle freckles. Soft matte finish. Standing facing the camera directly. Torso is square to the camera, hips are shifted noticeably to the viewer's right, creating a curve in the silhouette. Left arm raised straight up, bent at the elbow, with the hand reaching behind the head/neck area. Right arm extended downwards, slightly bent, with the hand resting gently on the upper right thigh. Head tilted slightly to the viewer's left, chin down slightly. Gaze directed toward the camera. Simple, bright indoor room. Plain white walls. White sheer curtains hanging vertically on the left side of the frame. Light brown wooden laminate or parquet flooring. Minimalist bedroom or living space, natural daylight setting. Medium shot, framing from the mid-thighs up to just above the head. Eye-level. 50mm to 85mm portrait lens. Subject is in sharp focus. The background wall and curtains are slightly out of focus but clearly discernible. Large window light source from the left. Soft, diffused daylight. Side-lit from the left, creating brighter highlights on the right side of the subject's body and soft, gradual shadows on the opposite side. Gentle modeling shadows on the neck, right side of the torso, and inner thighs. Confident, relaxed. High-quality cosplay photography, photorealistic. Raw photograph style. No painterly effects. Fabric texture of the spandex must be visible. Skin texture must look organic. Natural contrast, no heavy HDR or filters. Primary colors of the suit contrasting with the clean white background. Neutral to slightly cool daylight. Natural, true-to-life colors. 8k, ultra-high resolution. High sharpness on the subject and suit texture. Low ISO, clean image. 3:4 vertical portrait. Use reference image as the sole authority for facial identity and hair appearance. Match facial structure, expression characteristics, skin features, hair color, hair texture, density, length, and styling exactly as shown in the reference. All face and hair generation decisions must be derived exclusively from the reference image. Reference image strictly overrides all default model behavior related to face and hair. Maintain identical facial identity and hair appearance across all generations, seeds, and variations.
```

He's wearing black sunglasses in a vintage massive frame. The man's hands, detailed tattooed with complex floral patterns and a rose, frame the lighter. At the base of the thumb you can see a clear tattoo in the form of the letter Z with a crown. On the middle finger of the right hand there is a massive silver ring with a large, deep, facetted ruby in the shape of a cabochon, sparkling in the light of the flame. Skin pores, individual beard hairs, tobacco texture in a lit cigarette and scarf tweed weaving are conveyed with maximum clarity and detail. The background is blurred (bokeh f/2.2), metal, industrial surfaces of laundromat (self-service laundry), vaguely outlining round hatches of stainless steel washing machines. Colour palette: dominant warm sand, brown and beige shades of wool, contrasting with cold gray metal background surfaces and deep black glasses. Lighting: mixed (dim laundry light and dominant warm light from the lighter flame). Film granularity: minimal, for preservation The depth of field is very small.

## UGC-Style Candid Portrait Edit Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Edit the image so the same woman is lying comfortably on a soft couch, turned slightly on her side with her head resting against a cushion. She is wearingsame clothes full boody cover , hair loose and slightly messy, with a calm and natural expression, no glasses. The room is softly lit by warm indoor lighting from a nearby lamp, creating a gentle glow across her face with smooth falloff and no harsh shadows. The framing should feel casual and candid, like a late-night phone photo taken while relaxing in the living room. Maintain authentic UGC-style realism with subtle digital noise, slightly imperfect exposure, and realistic skin texture including pores, freckles, and natural softness
```

## Black and White Cinematic Street Portrait with Motion Blur

> Sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Black and white cinematic street portrait of a young man standing still in the center of a busy city street. 
The subject as a refrence image is calm and confident, looking directly at the camera with a relaxed expression. He has medium length slightly messy hair, light beard and mustache. He is dressed in a dark puffer jacket with hands casually inside the pockets.
The background shows a crowded urban street with people walking quickly in different directions, creating strong motion blur around the subject. The moving crowd appears streaked and ghostlike due to slow shutter speed, while the subject remains perfectly sharp and in focus.

Old style city buildings and street details are softly blurred in the background, giving a cinematic depth of field. The lighting is natural and diffused, creating soft highlights and shadows on the face. The image has a dramatic urban atmosphere with high contrast black and white tones.

Shot on a professional camera, 50mm lens, shallow depth of field, subject centered in frame, vertical composition, sharp focus on the subject, motion blur surrounding the subject, documentary street photography style, cinematic realism, high detail, professional photography, moody urban aesthetic.
```

Aspect Ratio & Framing:9:16 vertical aspect ratio.
Medium close-up portrait from upper chest to slightly above the head.
Subject centered vertically and horizontally.
Camera at eye level, straight-on perspective.
Leave intentional vertical negative negative space above and below for a cinematic social media look.
Dark studio background.
Pose & Expression:
Standing upright, shoulders squared, facing directly toward the camera.
Neutral, confident, slightly serious expression.
Eyes clearly visible and sharply focused, looking directly into the lens.
No glasses.
Outfit:
Black minimalist bomber jacket with a matte texture.
Plain black crew-neck t-shirt underneath.
Clean, modern styling.
Lighting:
High-contrast cinematic gel lighting.
Strong red light illuminating one side of the face and shoulder.
Strong blue light illuminating the opposite side.
Clear split-tone effect across the face.
Directional lighting sculpting cheekbones and jawline.
Deep shadows with controlled highlights.
Background remains nearly black.
Atmosphere:
Dense colored smoke drifting around the lower frame and slightly behind the shoulders.
Red smoke on the red-lit side.
Blue smoke on the blue-lit side.
Smoke must not obscure the face or eyes.
Moody, dramatic, music-video style ambience.
Camera & Technical Settings:
Professional full-frame camera look.
85mm lens.
f/1.8 aperture for shallow depth of field.
Sharp focus on eyes and skin texture.
Ultra high resolution.
Cinematic color grading with deep blacks, rich reds, and vibrant blues.
Studio-quality, high-detail, modern editorial finish.
No glasses. No extra accessories beyond the necklace. No distractions in the background.

A candid, high-resolution photograph capturing Sydney Sweeney on a different part of the same luxury superyacht teak deck from image_0.png, set against a similar dramatic, pine-covered cliff coastline and clear blue Mediterranean water. Sydney is in a relaxed standing pose, leaning against the polished steel railing near the stern, her wind-kissed (not soaking wet, but naturally textured) blonde hair flowing. She is wearing a textured, cream-colored, crocheted bikini top and matching high-waisted linen shorts with subtle gold trim. She has a genuine, relaxed laugh, looking slightly up and away, holding a clear glass with ice and water. Her sun-kissed skin shows natural texture. The composition is a medium shot, from the knees up, with a slightly different angle on the coastal cliffs and the other yacht from image_0.png, which is visible but smaller in the further distance. The golden hour sun casts a warm, soft glow, making the teak deck and details on the crocheted fabric rich in texture. A new, open book and a pair of chic tortoiseshell sunglasses are on a different curved curved bench. The water reflections are clear. No watermarks are present. The depth of field is shallow, focusing sharply on her expression and immediate surroundings, blurring the distant cliffs softly.
```

Create a vertical 9:16 miniature diorama scene inside a cozy vintage café with warm golden lighting and soft background bokeh.

In the center, place a realistic 3D chibi-style boy (large head, small body) with curly dark hair, a short beard, fair skin, and black rectangular glasses. Photorealistic 3D render with detailed textures (skin, fabric, wood, glass) and shallow depth of field, as if shot with a 35mm lens at f/1.8.

Cinematic 3D illustration of imagination coming to life. A cute young cartoon boy is stepping out of a spiral sketchbook placed on a warm wooden desk in a cozy creative workspace. The sketchbook page contains a pencil sketch drawing of the same boy, labeled at the top “MY WORLD – SKETCHES.” The drawing is simple graphite line art, while the real character emerging from it is rendered in fully detailed 3D animated style.

The boy looks identical to the drawing but transformed into a vibrant 3D character, with soft rounded facial features, big expressive eyes, fluffy brown hair, and a gentle friendly smile. He is wearing a casual raglan t-shirt (white with blue sleeves), denim jeans, and red sneakers.

One foot is still on the sketchbook page while the other steps onto the wooden desk, creating the illusion that he is coming to life directly from the drawing. The sketch lines subtly fade where the character emerges, blending 2D pencil art into 3D reality.

The desk around the notebook is filled with creative tools: scattered colored pencils, graphite pencils, pens, sketching tools, and erasers. A ceramic mug holding paintbrushes and pencils sits nearby with the text “CREATIVE TOOLS” printed on it.

The background shows a warm cozy artist studio environment, with soft bokeh fairy lights, shelves with books and art supplies, and a corkboard covered with colorful sticky notes pinned behind the desk. The lighting is warm golden evening light, creating cinematic depth, soft shadows, and glowing highlights.

Composition & camera
•slightly low angle perspective
•focus on the boy stepping forward
•shallow depth of field
•foreground desk details sharp, background softly blurred

Style
•Pixar / Disney-style 3D character design
•ultra detailed textures
•soft global illumination
•photoreal desk materials
•stylized animated character

Quality keywords
masterpiece, ultra detailed, 8k resolution, cinematic lighting, global illumination, depth of field, volumetric light, ray-traced reflections, warm color grading, storytelling composition, Pixar-quality 3D render, magical realism, imagination coming to life

⸻

Optional Negative Prompt (for diffusion models)
blurry, low resolution, distorted anatomy, extra limbs, bad hands, messy sketch lines, flat lighting, dull colors, watermark, text artifacts
```

## Photorealistic Cinematic Portrait of Woman in Pool

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Photorealistic, cinematic portrait, full body shot of a young Caucasian woman, mid-20s, with long wavy caramel brown hair cascading over her shoulders and down her back, subtle natural makeup, radiant fair skin with faint freckles across her nose and cheeks, wearing a pristine white string bikini with delicate gold O-ring details on the sides of the bottom. She is standing waist-deep in a bright blue tiled swimming pool, leaning forward slightly, her left hand resting gently on the light grey textured pool coping at the edge of the water. Her right arm is slightly bent, hand out of frame. She looks directly at the camera with a gentle, inviting expression.

The camera is positioned at a low angle, slightly looking up at the subject, at the level of her mid-torso. The background features a luxurious, modern white villa with dark brown wooden accents, including window frames, balconies, and roof eaves, under a clear azure sky with scattered soft white clouds. A white patio umbrella is visible in the mid-ground to the right, and white lounge chairs are faintly visible further in the background. Lush green tropical foliage subtly peeks out from behind the subject and the building.

The lighting is bright, natural direct sunlight from the upper left, creating strong highlights on her hair, shoulders, chest, and bikini fabric. Specular highlights catch the water droplets on her skin and the surface of the pool. The scene is illuminated with a warm, inviting glow.

Sharp focus on the subject with a shallow depth of field, rendering a soft, creamy bokeh in the background. Extreme detail on skin texture, hair strands, bikini stitching, and gold jewelry. Water appears clear and sparkling. Shot with a prime lens, possibly 85mm, f/1.8. High resolution, 4K, professional photography, octane render, global illumination, natural colors, vibrant, sunny atmosphere.
```

```
"A photorealistic, candid mirror selfie of three young women in a public bathroom, captured on a modern smartphone in high resolution 4k with a slight photographic grain, exuding a casual, playful, and youthful nightlife vibe. On the left is Sydney Sweeney with long wavy blonde hair, a neutral expression, and a slight pout. She is wearing a pink camisole with red lace trim, pink bottoms, and a brown braided O-ring belt, leaning her head affectionately on the middle woman's shoulder. In the middle is Megan Fox with long dark hair, smiling playfully with her tongue sticking out. She is wearing a white graphic tank top, blue denim shorts, and a thin gold belly chain, holding up a smartphone with a textured pink case and a beaded phone charm in her right hand to take the picture. On the right is Sabrina Carpenter with a blonde bob cut and bangs, smiling warmly. She is wearing a black halter top and light blue denim shorts, leaning in slightly with her arm resting near her hip. The background features white bathroom stall doors with slightly harsh reflections from the overhead indoor fluorescent lighting, which casts moderate shadows on their faces. A row of blue square tiles runs just below the mirror. The image is framed as a medium shot from mid-thigh up at an eye-level angle. Aspect ratio 4:5."
```

A hyper-realistic close-up shot at a 45-degree angle of a hand holding a miniature 3D diorama on a floating island representing {argument name="city name" default="Moscow, Russia"}. The base of the island is made of layered historic white stone and soil, with detailed textures and an elegant golden inscription “{argument name="city name uppercase" default="MOSCOW"}” on the front side.

At the top of the island, the main landmarks are depicted in extremely fine detail: the magnificent St. Basil’s Cathedral with its colorful twisted domes, the strict towers of the Moscow Kremlin, and the modern mirrored skyscrapers of Moscow City. Between them, miniature sightseeing riverboats can be seen on the curve of the Moskva River, along with the lush greenery of Zaryadye Park and its floating bridge.

Crystal-clear water from the Moskva River surrounds the island, showing soft reflections of city lights and tiny river trams. Decorative lilac bushes and red carnations grow along the edges of the island.

In the background, faint silhouettes of Red Square or the embankment at dusk are softly blurred due to cinematic depth of field. The lighting emphasizes the blue hour, with warm city streetlights, ultra-detailed architecture, and photorealistic textures.

Technical parameters:

Shot with an 85mm lens, f/1.8 aperture, shallow depth of field, HDR, global illumination, 16K resolution, hyper-realistic cinematic photography, realistic skin texture on the hand, masterpiece quality.
```

A highly realistic cinematic portrait of a young man standing outdoors during the daytime in a modern city street. Natural sunlight softly illuminates his face, creating gentle shadows and realistic skin texture with visible pores and fine details. He has slightly messy hair moving naturally in a light breeze and a calm confident expression while looking slightly away from the camera. He is wearing a casual modern outfit, a light jacket over a simple t shirt and dark jeans. The background shows a lively urban environment with buildings, trees, and people walking in the distance, softly blurred with shallow depth of field to keep focus on the subject.

The sunlight creates warm highlights on his hair and shoulders, giving a natural golden tone to the scene. The composition feels like professional street photography taken with a DSLR camera using an 85mm lens. The colors are natural and balanced, with realistic contrast and cinematic color grading.

Ultra photorealistic, cinematic lighting, high dynamic range, sharp focus on the eyes, natural skin tones, shallow depth of field, professional photography, 8k resolution, highly detailed, realistic daylight atmosphere.
```

Ultra-high-quality hyper-realistic photo featuring a real human and a giant 3D Pixar-style version of THE SAME person — both strictly based on the uploaded reference image. The real person and the Pixar character must have identical facial structure, hairstyle, facial expression, body proportions, height, and clothing. Both are wearing a knitted sweater with a gradient from light yellow to pink, high-waisted blue jeans, and white high-top sneakers. The pose must exactly match the reference: the real person stands relaxed with a friendly smile, with their hand naturally resting on the shoulder of the Pixar character. The Pixar version stands slightly wider, arms confidently positioned near the pockets, with a playful sly smirk, one raised eyebrow, and expressive Pixar-style eyes. The Pixar character should be proportionally larger, but anatomically identical to the real person. The facial expression, head tilt, posture, and body language must precisely replicate the reference image. Clean gray-blue studio background, soft even studio lighting, realistic shadows, crisp fabric texture, smooth Pixar-style skin shading, cinematic sharpness. DO NOT change the pose, reaction, clothing, or proportions. Strict adherence to the reference.
```

Cinematic portrait in golden hour, warm sunset light filtering through snow-capped Himalayan peaks. Start with a slow dolly-in shot from medium wide to close-up on a stunning young Kashmiri woman in her early 20s, gentle warm smile, expressive deep brown eyes, long dark wavy hair flowing softly in a light breeze. She wears an intricate traditional Kashmiri phiran in rich red with colorful embroidered floral and geometric patterns in gold, blue, and green, layered shawl, heavy silver jewelry including ornate maang tikka headpiece with mirror work and dangling beads, large jhumka earrings. She cradles a tiny adorable white baby goat kid with floppy ears and soft fur close to her chest, gently stroking its head; the little goat nuzzles contentedly against her, blinking curiously at the camera.

She stands in a lush alpine meadow dotted with vibrant pink and purple wildflowers, a woven basket of fresh flowers resting beside her feet. Majestic pine forests and dramatic jagged snow-covered mountains rise in the background under a clear blue sky with soft clouds. Gentle wind ripples through the grass and her dress. Soft natural diegetic sounds: distant bird calls, light wind, faint goat bleats. Shot on 35mm film, shallow depth of field, warm golden tones, high dynamic range, realistic yet dreamy cultural authenticity, serene joyful mood, 4K cinematic quality, smooth 24fps motion.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Photo of Person and Pixar Double

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-high-quality hyper-realistic photo showing a real person and a giant 3D Pixar-style version of the SAME person, both strictly based on the uploaded reference image. The real person and the Pixar character must have identical facial structure, hairstyle, facial expression, body proportions, height, and clothing. Both are wearing a knitted sweater with a gradient from light yellow to pink, high-waisted blue jeans, and white high-top sneakers. The pose must exactly match the reference: the real person stands relaxed with a friendly smile, with one hand naturally resting on the Pixar character’s shoulder. The Pixar version stands slightly wider, with hands confidently positioned near the pockets, with a playful mischievous smirk, a raised eyebrow, and expressive Pixar-style eyes. The size of the Pixar character should be proportionally larger but anatomically identical to the real person. The facial expression, head tilt, posture, and body language must precisely replicate the reference image. Clean grey-blue studio background, soft even studio lighting, realistic shadows, crisp fabric texture, smooth Pixar skin shading, cinematic sharpness. Do NOT change the pose, reaction, clothing, or proportions.

Negative prompt: incorrect clothing, different pose, different facial expression, mismatched face, exaggerated cartoon style, thin or bulky body, incorrect Pixar proportions, incorrect height ratio, smile instead of smirk, neutral face, realistic render instead of Pixar, distorted anatomy, different clothing colors, weak resemblance, generic Pixar face.
```

## Photorealistic Self-Portrait with Chibi and UI Overlay Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an Ultra-detailed, ratio 4:5, hyperrealistic full-body photograph of a stylish me posing on a modern city sidewalk under bright, direct midday sun. The composition is a vertical medium-full shot from an eye-level perspective. The me leans casually against a dark grey metal street pole that creates a strong vertical line just right of center. He wears a striking, slightly glossy crimson red faux leather jacket zipped halfway over a crisp white collared shirt. His trousers are wide-leg, cream-colored corduroy with visible texture. He wears polished black leather loafers. His pose is relaxed, with one leg crossed over the other, and his left hand is tucked into his pocket. He has curly black hair and wears modern black-framed glasses, looking off to his right with a neutral, cool expression. To his right, a cute chibi anime character version of himself, with a large head and big eyes, perfectly mirrors his pose and outfit, rendered with clean black line art and simple cell shading. On the left side of the frame, a large, semi-transparent, dark grey, rounded rectangle overlay mimicking an Apple Music player Ul is professionally superimposed, featuring album art of a red canoe on water and playback controls; this Ul has a subtle 'glassmorphism' frosted glass effect. The lighting is hard sunlight from the top-right, casting sharp, defined shadows on the light grey tiled pavement. The background consists of a modern building with large, greenish-tinted glass panels. Shot with a 35mm lens, f/5.6 aperture, creating sharp focus on the subject and graphics with a slightly soft background. The color science emphasizes high contrast between the crimson red, cream, and cool tones of the environment. Avoid any motion blur, unnatural skin textures, or distorted proportions. The integration of the graphic elements must be seamless and clean, without pixelation or rough edges.
```

## Cinematic Short Video Prompt for Baking Scene

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic short video of a woman baking sweets. Unified with film-like color tones, soft highlights, warm shadows, and shallow depth of field, the woman's cuteness is expressed only through gestures, subtle expressions, and reactions to sound. It begins with a close-up of her hands, depicting a series of actions—mixing ingredients, pouring, baking in the oven, and finally taking a bite—accompanied by ASMR-like immersive sounds (contact sounds of utensils, baking sounds, breathing). A warm video composed of smooth gimbal work and focus pulls, with natural transitions timed to the peak of movement and sound.
```

## Watercolor Spring Greeting Card Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, elle çizilmiş portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
STRICTLY preserve the appearance of the woman from the photo 1:1 — do not change facial features, proportions, age, skin texture, or individual characteristics. Do not stylize the face.
Create a spring greeting card.
A woman with thick, shiny hair falling freely over her shoulders. Her face is in a slight three-quarter profile, gaze directed upwards and to the right, with a warm, sincere smile.
She is wearing light yellow clothing and an airy yellow scarf that softly frames her face and neck, creating a sense of movement in the wind. In her hands is a bouquet of crocuses and hyacinths.
Background: a clear blue sky and slightly blurred stalks of tall grass, capturing the atmosphere of a fresh spring morning in nature.
Lighting is soft, natural, without harsh shadows.
Style — realistic watercolor greeting card: soft brushstrokes, rich but delicate colors, light paper texture. Neat natural makeup, pink lips, expressive eyebrows.
The composition is designed as a greeting card. Photorealism, high detail, warm spring mood.
```
